*Zihan Dong,Rui Qian,Qishi Zhan,Dongshen Peng,Kaixin Li,Yu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应预期策略树（AAPT），通过在GUI空闲时段预构建条件策略树，消除计算机使用智能体在瞬态GUI事件上的决策时延，将决策窗口内成功率从0.50提升至0.79（p=1.8×10⁻³），且不产生错误动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体常因瞬态GUI事件而失败，根本原因是昂贵的自回归解码位于决策关键路径上，导致正确动作产生时相关窗口已关闭。需要一种不修改底层模型即可消除该延迟的方法。

Method: AAPT采用双阶段机制：(1) 在屏幕空闲期间，利用冻结的多模态模型构建有界条件策略树，包含可观察守卫、预授权动作和分支专属截止期限，树的大小覆盖模型自身的解码延迟；(2) 事件发生时，轻量级观察器将变更门控帧与预建分支匹配，立即执行对应动作而无需生成新文本。

Result: 配对试验（预注册终点，精确McNemar检验）中，AAPT将成功率从0.50提升至0.79（p=1.8×10⁻³），无错误动作；开放循环和预测-重规划基线成功率均为零。在独立通用多模态模型上126次配对试验复现（p=4.9×10⁻¹³）。外部基准上与反应式基线整体表现相当但优势互补。预注册oracle探针推翻了最初假设，表明分支路由是因果瓶颈。

Conclusion: AAPT在可预先枚举候选动作的场景中表现最佳，而当动作无法预枚举时反应式执行更强。该方法通过将解码从决策关键时刻移出，显著提升瞬态事件处理能力，但路由准确性是核心约束。

Abstract: Computer-use agents often fail on transient GUI events because they produce the correct action only after the relevant window has already closed. We identify the main cause as expensive autoregressive decoding on the decision-time critical path. We propose Adaptive Anticipatory Policy Trees (AAPT), which eliminates this delay without modifying the underlying model. During idle screen periods, the same frozen multimodal model constructs a bounded conditional policy tree with observable guards, pre-authorized actions, and branch-specific deadlines. The tree is sized to cover the model's own decoding latency. When an event occurs, a lightweight observer matches change-gated frames to a prepared branch and immediately executes the corresponding action without generating new text. In paired trials with pre-registered endpoints and exact McNemar tests, AAPT improves the success rate from 0.50 to 0.79 within a contested decision window ($p=1.8\times10^{-3}$), while producing no incorrect actions. Both open-loop and predict-and-replan baselines achieve zero success because they still decode during execution. A preparation-time sweep shows that the gain emerges where the latency-based tree-sizing rule predicts, and ablations reveal three key requirements: fast observer decoding, valid tree planning, and accurate branch routing. A pre-registered oracle probe rejects our initial hypothesis and instead points to branch routing as the causal bottleneck. We further reproduce the effect on an independent general-purpose multimodal model over 126 paired trials ($p=4.9\times10^{-13}$). On an external benchmark, AAPT matches the overall performance of a reactive baseline, although the two methods exhibit complementary strengths. Together, these results suggest that AAPT performs best when candidate actions can be enumerated in advance, whereas reactive execution remains stronger when they cannot.

</details>


### [223] [QQWorld: Quantile-Quantile Matching for World Model Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28415)
*Zhoushun Yu,Xiaoyu Hu,Xiangyu Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QQWorld，用分位数-分位数（QQ）匹配目标替代Epps-Pulley（EP）正则化目标，以解决潜伏世界模型（LeWorldModel）中EP梯度在尾部样本上快速消失的问题，从而改善潜变量分布的高斯对齐质量并提升规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜伏世界模型在压缩表示空间中进行高效规划，其性能高度依赖于学习的潜变量分布质量。LeWorldModel使用EP目标将潜变量正则化为各向同性高斯分布，但EP的校正梯度对孤立尾部样本迅速消失，导致重尾偏差得不到有效控制，影响模型性能。

Method: 提出QQWorld，用分位数-分位数匹配目标替代EP目标，直接将投影后的潜样本与秩匹配的高斯分位数对齐，从而在尾部维持有效的校正梯度。此外，提出跨批次QQ（cross-batch QQ），利用前序批次的分离样本来扩大有效排序池，并刻画其偏差-方差权衡。

Result: 在四个控制环境中，QQWorld有效提升了LeWM的平均规划成功率，同时获得更好的高斯对齐程度和更薄的潜变量尾部。

Conclusion: QQWorld通过分位数匹配的正则化策略，解决了EP目标在尾部梯度消失的缺陷，显著改善了潜伏世界模型的潜分布质量与规划性能，为潜伏世界模型的分布正则化提供了更优的替代方案。

Abstract: Latent world models enable efficient planning by predicting future states in a compact representation space, but their performance depends critically on the quality of the learned latent distribution. LeWorldModel (LeWM) regularizes its latents toward an isotropic Gaussian using the Epps-Pulley (EP) objective. We show that the corrective gradients of EP rapidly vanish for isolated tail samples, leaving heavy-tailed deviations insufficiently controlled. To address this limitation, we propose QQWorld, which replaces EP with a quantile-quantile matching objective that directly aligns projected latent samples with rank-matched Gaussian quantiles, thereby maintaining effective corrective gradients in the tails. We further develop cross-batch QQ, which enlarges the effective ranking pool using detached samples from previous batches, and characterize its bias-variance trade-off. Across four control environments, QQWorld effectively improves the average planning success rate of LeWM, while consistently yielding better Gaussian alignment and thinner latent tails.

</details>


### [224] [QAdapt: A Noise-Adaptive Neural Pre-Decoding Framework for Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28422)
*Ran Miao,Rui Luo,Xiaohan Shan,Xiaoming Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QAdapt是一个噪声自适应的神经预解码框架，用于表面码量子纠错。它捕获综合征数据中的局部时空相关性，顺序适应不断演变的噪声条件并缓解灾难性遗忘，将残余综合征转发给传统全局解码器。在110种合成分布外噪声配置中一致降低逻辑错误率，在Google Willow基准数据上无需目标域微调即可将逻辑错误率降低5.79%、后端解码延迟降低9.32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算受限于物理噪声和经典解码器处理快速生成的综合征数据的延迟。硬件噪声具有强、异构和非平稳特性，且仿真到硬件的分布偏移会显著降低固定的神经解码器性能，因此需要能够自适应演进噪声条件的解码框架。

Method: QAdapt框架包含三个关键设计：(1)捕获综合征数据中的局部时空相关性；(2)顺序适应演化中的噪声条件，同时通过特定机制缓解灾难性遗忘；(3)将残余综合征转发给传统全局解码器进行后续处理。

Result: 在110种合成分布外噪声配置下，QAdapt相对于神经预解码基线一致降低了逻辑错误率。在Google Willow基准数据上，无需目标域微调即实现了逻辑错误率最高5.79%和残差综合征后端解码延迟最高9.32%的降低。

Conclusion: QAdapt提供了一种实用且与解码器兼容的方法，可在演进的硬件噪声条件下提高量子纠错的鲁棒性和后端解码效率，验证了其在实际量子硬件上的有效性。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing (FTQC) relies on quantum error correction to suppress physical errors and preserve logical information at scale. In practice, however, performance is constrained not only by physical noise but also by the latency of classical decoders processing rapidly generated syndrome data. This challenge is exacerbated by hardware noise that is strong, heterogeneous, and nonstationary, as well as by the simulation-to-hardware distribution shift that can substantially degrade fixed neural decoders. We present QAdapt, a noise-adaptive neural pre-decoding framework for surface-code quantum error correction. QAdapt captures local spatiotemporal correlations in syndrome data, sequentially adapts to evolving noise conditions while mitigating catastrophic forgetting, and forwards the residual syndrome to a conventional global decoder. Across 110 synthetic out-of-distribution noise configurations for rotated surface-code memory circuits, QAdapt consistently reduces the logical error rate relative to the neural pre-decoding baseline. On Google's Willow benchmark data, without target-domain fine-tuning, it achieves reductions of up to 5.79 percent in logical error rate and 9.32 percent in backend decoding latency on the residual syndrome. These results demonstrate that QAdapt provides a practical and decoder-compatible approach to improving the robustness and backend decoding efficiency of quantum error correction under evolving hardware noise.

</details>


### [225] [Kohn-Sham Spectral Embedding on Sparse Graphs at the Nishimori Temperature for Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28428)
*V. S. Usatyuk,D. A. Sapozhnikov,S. I. Egorov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kohn-Sham谱嵌入（KSSE），一种受物理学启发的能量基模型，用稀疏图谱嵌入替代密集CNN分类器。通过将预训练特征映射到准循环LDPC图并构造正则化拉普拉斯算子作为Kohn-Sham哈密顿量，在Nishimori温度下求解谱问题，在ImageNet-1000上以约2124万参数实现88.93%的Top-1准确率，超越Swin-L和ViT-H/14，模型规模分别缩减10倍和30倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统密集CNN分类器参数量庞大且计算开销高，本文旨在通过物理学启发的稀疏图谱嵌入方法，在保持甚至提升分类精度的同时显著降低模型参数量和计算复杂度，解决深度学习模型效率与性能之间的权衡问题。

Method: KSSE将预训练特征映射到准循环LDPC图，构造正则化拉普拉斯作为Kohn-Sham哈密顿量，在Nishimori温度下求解D个独立通道的谱问题（复杂度O(N log N + k²_mode N)），利用FFT处理循环块和低阶Rayleigh精化。图拓扑通过星域手术优化，构造保持信息码字的局部凸性同时限制残差挫折。还引入多尺度分形分析验证从粗糙区域到星域盆地的景观转变。

Result: KSSE在ImageNet-1000上的转导协议中实现88.93% Top-1准确率，仅用约2124万参数，超越Swin-L（197M，86.4-87.3%），与ViT-H/14（632M，88.0-89.5%）相当，参数规模分别缩减10倍和30倍。同时提出六项理论结果，涵盖Ihara-Bass恒等式、陷阱集特征值对应、加性通道可分离性、手术定理、拟平稳扰动界和不动点收敛定理。

Conclusion: KSSE证明了物理启发的谱嵌入方法在视觉分类任务中能以极小的参数规模匹敌乃至超越大规模Transformer模型，展示了图论、统计物理与深度学习交叉融合的巨大潜力，为高效模型设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: We introduce Kohn--Sham Spectral Embedding (KSSE), a physics-inspired energy-based model replacing dense CNN classifiers with a sparse-graph spectral embedding evaluated at the Nishimori temperature of an associated Random-Bond Ising Model. By mapping pre-trained features onto quasi-cyclic low-density parity-check graphs and constructing a regularized Laplacian acting as a Kohn--Sham Hamiltonian, we solve $D$ independent channel spectral problems in $\mathcal{O}(N\log N + k^2_{\text{mode}} N)$ time via FFT on circulant blocks (leveraging Pontryagin self-duality of $\mathbb{Z}/p\mathbb{Z}$) and low-order Rayleigh refinement. Graph topology is optimized using \emph{star-domain surgery}: rather than destroying information-carrying codewords by removing frustrated cycles, we construct edge shifts creating local convexity around codewords while bounding residual frustration to $ρ(B_γ)\leq 1+δ$. Multi-scale fractal analysis ($D_2$ spectrum) and fractal learning-rate landscape certifies a landscape transition from rough regimes ($D_2>3$) to star-domain basins ($D_2<1$), enabling Rayleigh refinement with $k_{\text{mode}}=5$ modes. We prove six theoretical results: a generalized Ihara--Bass identity linking belief propagation to the Laplacian; trapping-set eigenvalue correspondence; additive channel separability with an explicit exchange-correlation bound; a surgery theorem bounding frustration with attractor width $Ω(1/\sqrt{d_{\min}})$; a quasi-stationarity perturbation bound; and a fixed-point convergence theorem. In a transductive protocol on ImageNet-1000 with frozen EfficientNet-B4 features ($D=1792$), KSSE achieves \textbf{88.93\%} Top-1 accuracy using $\approx 21.24$M parameters, outperforming Swin-L (197M, 86.4--87.3\%) and matching ViT-H/14 (632M, 88.0--89.5\%) under standard inductive setups, while reducing model footprint by $10\times$ and $30\times$, respectively.

</details>


### [226] [Oracle-Budgeted Molecular Optimization with Short-Term Graph Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28437)
*Jiannan Yang,Veronika Thost,Xiang Ling,Tengfei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出短程图记忆（short-term graph memory）模块，作为即插即用组件，在不改变生成器架构和原更新规则的前提下，通过在线图神经网络预筛选候选分子，优化有限的oracle预算分配，显著提升分子优化的top-10得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子优化通常在有限的oracle预算下进行，因此"评估什么"与"生成什么"同等重要。现有方法忽视了对有限oracle查询预算的智能分配，导致预算浪费在低效用分子上。

Method: 提出短程图内存模块：维护一个在线图神经网络代理模型，在每一轮生成后对候选池进行预筛选，优先将有限oracle预算分配给预测效用更高的分子。该模块不改变原有生成器架构和更新规则，仅作为外部筛选插件工作。

Result: 在标准分子优化基准上，应用于基于片段的生成器时，平均top-10得分在无额外oracle成本下得到提升，且在任一oracle上均不劣于基线；在1000次调用的tight预算下，该增益扩展至全部四种测试的生成器。在大预算下，其收益与骨干网络的搜索广度和原生利用oracle反馈的效率一致。

Conclusion: 提供了一种简单有效的方式来更精准地分配固定oracle预算，并揭示了哪些类型的生成器能从中受益，即搜索范围广且原生利用反馈效率较低的生成器获益更大。

Abstract: Molecular optimization is commonly performed under a limited oracle budget, which makes deciding what to evaluate as important as deciding what to generate. We introduce short-term graph memory, a plug-in module that preserves the generator architecture and native update rule while learning from previously evaluated molecules to prioritize subsequent oracle queries. The module maintains an online graph neural surrogate that pre-screens each round's candidate pool, so the fixed oracle budget is spent on molecules with higher predicted utility. Applied to a fragment-based generator on a standard molecular optimization benchmark, it improves the mean top-10 score at no extra oracle cost and never falls behind the base on any oracle; the gain extends to all four generators we tested at a tight budget of one thousand calls. We then analyze how surrogate-guided selection interacts with the exploration and exploitation behavior of different generators. Its benefit at larger budgets is consistent with two properties of the backbone: how broadly it searches, and how effectively its native search already exploits oracle feedback. We provide a simple way to spend a fixed oracle budget more selectively, and evidence on which generators benefit from it.

</details>


### [227] [Cybersecurity Detection Classification with Reasoning-enabled Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28460)
*Amol Khanna,Manu Nandan,Cristian Viorel Popa,Joan Pujol-Roig,Diana Bolocan,Laura Vasilie,Alexandru Apostu,Chase Helwig,Mihaela Gaman,Michael Brautbar,Edward Raff,Chase Midler,Sven Krasser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对安全运营中心(SOC)的警报疲劳问题，提出了一种结合链式思维推理的警报分流分类器。系统通过在真实人工标注的Windows端点检测数据上训练，结合自动提示优化、自训练和带可验证奖励的强化学习，并单独训练一个校准器读取完整推理轨迹以估计判定正确性概率。系统达到82.6%测试准确率，在高置信度工作点上良性召回率提升43.0%，恶意召回率提升18.3%。同时发现未训练的置信度评判器会导致高置信度召回率归零，且微调的30B模型显著优于前沿通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SOC面临严重的警报疲劳问题，检测数量远超工作人员当日可处理量。现有工作虽然直接提示或微调大型语言模型输出分流标签，但未训练模型推理检测是否为真正的威胁，缺乏对检测背后的因果推理能力，导致分流决策缺少可解释性和可靠性。

Method: 该方法结合了自动提示优化、自训练和带可验证奖励的强化学习，在真实人工标注的Windows端点检测数据上训练链式思维推理能力的分流分类器。由于CoT推理会降低标签token概率，作者在LLM之后单独训练一个校准器，该校准器读取完整推理轨迹并估计判定正确的概率，从而在高置信度工作点实现自动化分流。

Result: 系统达到82.6%的测试准确率；在高置信度自动化分流工作点上，良性召回率提升43.0%，恶意召回率提升18.3%（相对直接标签LLM分类器）。研究表明未经训练的置信度评判器会使高置信度召回率崩溃至零，而微调的30B模型显著超过前沿通用模型，验证了定向训练优于单纯扩大模型规模。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在安全分流场景中，链式思维推理结合专门的校准机制能够显著提升自动化分流的准确性和召回率。同时指出针对特定领域任务的定向微调比依赖更大规模的前沿通用模型更有效，为SOC自动化警报处理提供了可落地的实践路径。

Abstract: A major issue in Security Operations Centers (SOCs) is alert fatigue, as the number of detections reported is more than staff can triage in a given day. Prior work prompts or fine-tunes large language models (LLMs) to emit a triage label directly, but does not train them to reason about whether a detection is a genuine threat. We train a chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning-enabled triage classifier on real, human-labeled Windows endpoint detections by combining automated prompt optimization, self-training, and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. We find that CoT reasoning also degrades the label-token probabilities that automated triage relies on, so we separately train a calibrator that reads the full reasoning trace and estimates the probability that the verdict is correct. Our system reaches 82.6% test accuracy and, at the high-confidence operating point that governs automated triage, improves benign recall by 43.0% and malicious recall by 18.3% over a direct-label LLM classifier. We further show that the trained calibrator is necessary - an untrained confidence judge collapses high-confidence recall to zero - and that a finetuned 30B model significantly outperforms frontier general-purpose models, motivating targeted training over scale.

</details>


### [228] [Stage-Replay Divergence Follows the KV Cache: Fixed-Prefix Precision Controls and Bidirectional Cache Transplantation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28495)
*Alexander Boesgaard Lorup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对"阶段重放诊断"的核心假设——即用相同token重新预填可以视为从原始解码器状态继续——进行了严格审计。通过200项配对实验、固定前缀2x2设计、改进桥接方法及双向全层K/V移植实验，证明精确token重放虽可重复，但无法保真保留实时缓存状态；在测试状态下，边界K/V缓存是分歧轨迹的因果充分载体，数值精度则调节其行为表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阶段重放诊断方法广泛应用于分析推理系统，其隐含假设是将新鲜预填的token前缀视为与原始解码器状态产生的延续在功能上等价。然而该假设的因果有效性从未被系统性地审计——即精确重放是否真正保留了实时解码器状态的保真度，以及这一保真度差异是否会影响下游输出的行为表现。

Method: 本研究采用多层实验设计：(1) 200项配对实验，比较保留的实时缓存与一次性预填相同整数token，并在两侧放置完全相同的副本；(2) BF16下检验重放构造对输出后缀和正确性标签的影响；(3) 固定前缀2x2设计，控制全部200个token状态，交叉构造方式与数值精度（BF16 vs FP32）；(4) 提出"前瞻桥接"方法，实现逐token增量式重放与保留实时缓存的位级精确一致性；(5) 双向移植全部48层键/值缓存，验证其因果充分性；(6) 在后续检查点进行结果盲复制验证。

Result: 主要发现：BF16下重放构造在166个后缀和20个正确性标签上产生分歧，但准确率差异仅1点（配对95%CI [-3.5, +5.5]）；FP32下无任何解码分歧（95% Wilson上界1.88%）；前瞻桥接使12/12行达到位级精确；全部48层K/V双向移植使得所有测试的分歧延续遵循其缓存捐赠者（初检点24/24，盲复制43/43）。

Conclusion: 精确token重放可以具备良好的可重复性，但不保证保留实时状态的因果保真度。边界K/V缓存在所测试状态下是分歧轨迹的因果充分载体，而数值精度（BF16 vs FP32）会调节该因果效应的行为表达。这为评估AI推理系统诊断方法的有效性提供了重要的方法论警示。

Abstract: Stage-replay diagnostics reconstruct intermediate token prefixes and treat fresh-prefill continuation as continuation from the decoder state that originally reached the prefix. We audit that assumption at a whole reasoning-stage boundary in a Qwen2.5-derived system. A matched 200-item experiment compares retained live cache with one-shot prefill of identical integer tokens and places an exact replica on both sides. In BF16, replicas remain exact while the constructions differ on 166 suffixes and 20 correctness labels; the accuracy difference is only one point (paired 95% CI [-3.5, +5.5]). A fixed-prefix 2x2 holds all 200 token states constant while crossing construction and precision. The BF16 disagreements recur, whereas FP32 produces no decoded disagreement (95% Wilson upper bound 1.88%). A prospective bridge makes token-by-token incremental and retained live caches bit-exact on 12/12 rows; an all-200 saved-ledger audit reproduces every retained trajectory and comparison fingerprint. Bidirectional transplantation of all 48 key/value layers makes every tested divergent continuation follow its cache donor, both on a selected set at the primary checkpoint (24/24) and an outcome-blind replication at a later checkpoint (43/43). Exact-token replay can therefore be repeatable without preserving live-state fidelity. On the tested states, boundary K/V cache is a causally sufficient carrier of the divergent trajectory, while numerical precision moderates its behavioral expression.

</details>


### [229] [The Role of Causality in Algorithmic Recourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28497)
*Srikanth Avasarala,Varun Gupta,Shahin Jabbari,Saber Salehkaleybar,Juba Ziani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于因果表演性框架（causal performative framework）的算法回溯（algorithmic recourse）方法，解决现有回溯方法只关注翻转模型预测而忽略真实资质改善的问题。通过结构因果模型建模回溯动作的传播效应，在非凸优化条件下刻画了表演性稳定解的存在性，并在半合成与真实信贷数据集上验证了因果回溯策略优于标准经验风险最小化，减少重复训练需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法回溯方法仅致力于翻转模型的预测结果，未考虑推荐动作是否能真正改善个体的真实资质（true qualifications），还是仅仅使个体操纵（game）分类器。这导致部署的回溯策略会引发有偏行为响应（misaligned behavioral responses），降低预测准确性，并在模型重新训练后失效。作者旨在从因果层面形式化并解决这一失败模式。

Method: 作者构建了一个因果表演性框架（causal performative framework），通过结构因果模型（SCM）刻画回溯动作如何传播至特征之间的交互以及真实标签。该框架下优化问题即使在凸损失下也呈现非凸性。作者表征了表演性稳定解（performatively stable solutions）存在且可通过简单迭代动力学高效计算的条件，并对比了忽略因果结构的回溯策略与因果感知回溯策略的行为差异。

Result: 在信用半合成与真实数据集上的实验表明：忽略因果结构的回溯策略会诱发大规模且有偏的行为响应；因果回溯策略则能达到稳定的均衡，减少博弈激励（gaming incentives）。该方法的性能持续优于标准经验风险最小化，同时减少了因策略性主体行为导致的分布偏移而引发的重复模型重训练需求。

Conclusion: 因果结构对于算法回溯的有效性至关重要。将因果信息纳入回溯策略不仅能改善个体的真实资质，还能实现表演性稳定均衡、降低操纵激励，从而提升模型的长期预测性能并减少重训练开销。

Abstract: Algorithmic recourse aims to provide individuals with actionable changes to improve their predicted outcomes in high-stakes classification settings, such as loan and mortgage applications. However, most existing approaches focus only on flipping a model's prediction, without accounting for whether the recommended changes lead to genuine improvement in an individual's true qualifications or merely enable strategic gaming of the classifier. Consequently, deployed recourse policies can induce behavioral responses that degrade predictive accuracy and become ineffective after model retraining.
  In this work, we formalize this failure mode through a causal performative framework for recourse. We model how recourse actions propagate through a structural causal model, capturing interactions among features as well as their effect on the true label. These causal responses induce a non-convex optimization problem, even under standard convex losses. We characterize conditions under which performatively stable solutions exist and can be efficiently computed via simple iterative dynamics. Our analysis reveals that recourse policies that ignore causal structure can induce large, misaligned behavioral responses, whereas causal recourse leads to stable equilibria that reduce incentives for gaming. Experiments on both semi-synthetic and real credit datasets demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms standard empirical risk minimization while reducing the need for repeated model retraining to accommodate distribution shifts caused by strategic agent behavior.

</details>


### [230] [Same Graph Cross-Task Transfer in GNNs: Protocols and Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28525)
*Neelam Akula,Surbhi Kumar,Murat Kantarcioglu,Baris Coskunuzer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了同图上的节点分类（NC）与链接预测（LP）跨任务迁移问题，提出了无泄漏的评估协议，发现NC→LP在同质图上稳定有益，而LP→NC仅在结构主导且LP容易、NC未饱和时可靠，并提出了CoTask Score（CTS）来评估共享编码器下的联合效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图学习评估常常使用不兼容的数据划分、不同的观测图假设和不同的负采样规则，导致关于同一图上跨任务（NC与LP）迁移的结论不可靠，亟需一个规范化的评估框架。

Method: 提出无泄漏协议：固定节点和边划分、使用排除被评估边的共享消息传递图、LP使用固定负样本。在三种骨干网络（GCN、GraphSAGE、GPS）上进行系统实验，并引入CoTask Score（CTS）以及基于同质性等数据集统计量来指导机制选择。

Result: 迁移方向性强且可预测：NC→LP在同质图上一致有益；LP→NC即使简单重用表征也可能降低精度，仅在结构主导（LP容易且NC未饱和）时可靠为正；同质性等简单统计量可有效指导迁移机制选择。

Conclusion: 同图NC-LP跨任务迁移具有明显方向性，NC→LP可视为稳定的预训练策略，LP→NC仅在特定条件下作为结构预训练有价值；CTS和同质性指标可用于避免负迁移、指导架构与训练机制选择。

Abstract: Many real-world graphs support multiple predictive tasks over the same underlying structure, creating an opportunity to reuse supervision across node classification (NC) and link prediction (LP). However, existing evaluations often rely on incompatible splits, observed-graph assumptions, and negative sampling rules, making conclusions about same-graph cross-task transfer unreliable. We formalize same-graph NC-LP transfer and propose a leakage-free protocol that fixes node and edge splits, uses a shared message-passing graph that excludes evaluated edges, and employs fixed negatives for LP. Across three backbones (GCN, GraphSAGE, GPS), we find that transfer is strongly directional and predictable: NC $\to$ LP is consistently beneficial on homophilic graphs, while LP $\to$ NC is fragile and can even degrade accuracy under naive representation reuse. LP $\to$ NC becomes reliably positive mainly in a structure-dominant regime where LP is easy but NC is unsaturated, suggesting that LP acts as structural pretraining. Finally, we introduce the CoTask Score (CTS) to summarize joint NC+LP utility when a shared encoder must serve both tasks, and show that simple dataset statistics, especially homophily, can guide mechanism choice and help avoid negative transfer.

</details>


### [231] [APO: Unsupervised Atomic Policy Optimization for 3D Structure Prediction of Atomic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28553)
*Shentong Mo,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出APO（Atomic Policy Optimization），一种完全无监督的3D原子结构对齐框架，通过双奖励机制（本征模态强化与热力学稳定性）和组相对策略优化，无需地面真值坐标即可对齐流匹配模型，在晶体和抗体结构预测上超越有监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流匹配模型（如FlowDPO）在3D原子结构预测中依赖监督偏好学习来对齐地面真值坐标，但实验标注成本极高，尤其对于新型晶体相或从头设计的蛋白质，在数据稀缺场景下形成瓶颈。

Method: APO将组相对策略优化（GRPO）适配到3D原子环境，提出双奖励机制：(1) 通过样本相似度的特征分解，强化策略的主导潜在结构模态；(2) 强制热力学稳定性。模型在采样组内通过识别物理上合理的构型实现"自我纠正"。

Result: 在晶体和抗体结构预测基准上，APO一致性地超越全监督基线，在匹配率和结构保真度上达到新的最先进水平；同时有效拉直概率路径，显著提升推理效率。

Conclusion: 内在物理一致性可以替代有噪声的监督坐标匹配，作为更优的对齐指导信号，为数据稀缺场景下的结构建模提供了无监督范式。

Abstract: Predicting the 3D structures of atomic systems is fundamental to advancing material science and drug discovery. While flow-matching models (, FlowDPO) have recently shown promise in this domain, their performance relies heavily on alignment with ground-truth coordinates via supervised preference learning. However, obtaining experimental labels for novel crystal phases or de novo proteins is prohibitively expensive, creating a bottleneck for structural modeling in data-scarce regimes. In this work, we propose (Atomic Policy Optimization), a fully unsupervised alignment framework that eliminates the need for ground-truth reference structures. APO adapts group-relative policy optimization to 3D atomic environments, utilizing a novel dual-reward mechanism: (i) a that reinforces the policy's dominant latent structural modes through eigen-decomposition of sample similarities, and (ii) a that enforces thermodynamic stability. Our framework enables the model to ``self-correct'' by identifying physically plausible configurations within sampled groups. Extensive benchmarks on crystal and antibody structure prediction demonstrate that APO consistently outperforms fully supervised baselines, achieving a new state-of-the-art in match rates and structural fidelity. Furthermore, we show that APO effectively straightens probability paths, significantly improving inference efficiency. Our results suggest that intrinsic physical consistency can serve as a superior guide for alignment compared to noisy, supervised coordinate matching.

</details>


### [232] [$β$-OPSD: Deriving with Policy Optimization, Training with Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28582)
*Jiawei Xu,Minghui Liu,Juzheng Zhang,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出β-OPSD方法，将vanilla OPSD（β=1的特例）推广为可调正则化参数的一般形式，通过几何插值参考策略与特权教师，并将闭式解转化为token级logits混合的蒸馏目标，在不牺牲效率的前提下提升推理语言模型的优化稳定性和下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vanilla OPSD虽然对提升推理语言模型有前景，但在实际中不稳定、需要大量工程调优。作者发现其不稳定的结构根源在于它是一个固定β=1的特殊策略优化成员，缺乏可控制的KL正则化参数来权衡参考策略锚定与特权教师指导。

Method: 1) 将OPSD推广为β-OPSD，引入可调KL惩罚权重β，推导其最优策略为参考策略与特权教师之间的几何插值；2) 不直接优化RL目标（成本高、方差大），而是利用闭式解构造蒸馏目标；3) 不同β对应参考-教师路径上的不同目标点，通过混合token级logits高效实现；4) 引入return-to-go credit assignment使token更新与序列级目标对齐。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的实验表明，β-OPSD在优化稳定性和下游推理性能上均持续优于vanilla OPSD。

Conclusion: β-OPSD提供了一条从自蒸馏到策略优化再回到蒸馏的原则性路径，既保留了OPSD的高效实用性，又通过可控的β参数实现了更好的性能与稳定性。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is a promising approach to improve reasoning language models, but it remains brittle in practice: making it work reliably often requires substantial engineering effort. We identify a structural source of this difficulty: vanilla OPSD is precisely the $β=1$ member of a broader policy-optimization family, where $β$ weights the KL penalty anchoring the student to a reference policy. This equivalence turns $β$ from an implicit value fixed at one into a controllable regularization parameter, yielding a more general formulation that trades off proximity to a reference policy against privileged teacher guidance. We introduce $β$-OPSD and derive its optimal policy as a geometric interpolation between the reference policy and the privileged teacher. Directly optimizing this objective with reinforcement learning, however, would be costly and high-variance. Rather than optimize the RL objective directly, we turn its closed-form solution into a distillation target. Each value of $β$ selects a target along the reference-to-teacher path, which we implement efficiently by mixing their token-level logits. In this way, inexpensive distillation approximates the solution of expensive policy optimization. Return-to-go credit assignment further aligns token updates with the sequence-level objective while retaining the simplicity of OPSD. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that $β$-OPSD consistently outperforms vanilla OPSD, improving optimization stability and downstream reasoning performance. Our results provide a principled route from self-distillation to policy optimization and back without sacrificing the efficiency that makes OPSD practical.

</details>


### [233] [KAISEN: Reproducible Subgroup Fairness Auditing for Clinical Risk Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28608)
*Sparsh Roy,Samuel Girmachew,Nishita Chavan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KAISEN，一个包含五个阶段的临床风险模型审计流水线，并在具有已知真实标签的合成基准（16个疾病任务、15个社会决定因素轴、3个预设交叉）上评估至失败点。发现：(i)显著性统计量与原始EOD的相关性受最小可检测效应影响且可通过标准化提高；(ii)按组阈值优化持续降低EOD而Platt缩放对EOD无显著效果；(iii)机制诊断在受控条件下100%正确但在代理错误设定下完全失效且无失效信号；(iv)CUSUM失效和误报主要由队列种子驱动而非疾病，参考阈值无法跨队列迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床风险模型在整体上表现良好，但对不同患者亚群可能产生显著不同的错误率。已有审计流水线虽被提出用于检测此类公平性问题，但其各个组件很少被压力测试，导致审计系统内部哪些部分值得信任、在何种条件下可靠均不明确。

Method: 构建KAISEN五阶段审计流水线：(1)亚组分层、(2)差异度量（基于EOD）、(3)机制诊断、(4)事后缓解（按组阈值优化与组级Platt缩放对比）、(5)漂移监测（CUSUM）。在16个疾病任务、15个社会决定因素轴（来自Healthy People 2030）及3个预定交叉构成的合成基准上系统评估，所有场景具有已知真实标签。

Result: (i)显著性计数与原始EOD的秩相关为ρ=0.56，经最小可检测效应标准化后升至ρ=0.78；(ii)按组阈值优化在48/48个保留数据集上降低EOD（配对Δ=-0.285，95%CI[-0.313,-0.252]），而Platt缩放在19/48个运行中改善EOD（效果近乎为零，行为如掷硬币）；(iii)机制诊断在144/144个受控案例中正确分类，但在代理错误设定下48个模型驱动案例全部无法恢复，且无任何失效信号；(iv)在参考阈值下，27个误报全部和8个漏检中的7个来自不同随机种子（卡方p=0.002），说明CUSUM阈值跨队列不可迁移。

Conclusion: 审计流水线各组件需分别验证而非整体信任：某些步骤（如按组阈值优化）稳健可靠，而另一些（如机制诊断和跨队列漂移监测阈值）在特定条件下会静默失效。审计报告应关注方差而非平均值（如Platt缩放的EOD效果）。所有结果基于合成数据，不构成临床有效性的直接证据。

Abstract: Clinical risk models routinely achieve strong aggregate performance while producing materially different error rates across patient subgroups. Audit pipelines have been proposed to catch this, but their components are rarely stress-tested, so it is unclear which parts of an audit can be trusted and under what conditions. We present KAISEN, a five-phase audit pipeline covering subgroup stratification, disparity measurement, mechanism diagnostics, post-hoc mitigation, and drift monitoring, evaluated to the point of failure on a synthetic benchmark of 16 disease tasks, 15 social-determinant axes from Healthy People 2030, and three prespecified intersections. Four findings follow. (i) Significance tracks each axis's gap against its own minimum detectable effect: rank correlation between significance count and raw equalized-odds difference (EOD) across the 15 axes is rho = 0.56, rising to rho = 0.78 once EOD is standardized by that floor. (ii) Per-group threshold optimization reduces EOD in 48 of 48 held-out runs (paired delta = -0.285, 95% CI [-0.313, -0.252]), while group-wise Platt scaling -- the better calibrator -- behaves as a coin flip on EOD (19 of 48 runs improved, 95% CI [0.26, 0.55]) with mean effect near zero, so what an audit should report is the variance, not the average. (iii) The mechanism diagnostic classifies 144 of 144 controlled cases correctly but recovers none of 48 model-driven cases under proxy misspecification, with no signal that it failed. (iv) CUSUM failures and false alarms track cohort realization far more than disease: at the reference threshold, all 27 false alarms and 7 of 8 missed shifts come from different seeds (chi-squared p = 0.002), so a threshold tuned on one cohort fails to transfer. All results are synthetic with known ground truth and do not establish clinical validity. Code, artifacts, and scripts reproducing every number are released.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [234] [Entropy-Smooth Convex Optimization Cannot Be Accelerated](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27476)
*Jacob M. Aguirre,Dmitrii M. Ostrovskii*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在凸且关于负熵相对L-光滑的函数类别中，最小化问题的收敛率存在Ω(L/T)的下界，该下界对d=Ω(T²)情况下的所有一阶方法均成立，表明镜像下降在该类别中在日志因子内是最优的。结果还扩展到量子设置，即关于负冯·诺依曼熵相对L-光滑的函数类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前已有工作（Dragomir et al., 2022）证明在相对光滑假设下加速可能不可能，但其使用的代理函数是病理性的且与困难实例一起构造。本文旨在更自然地展示非加速性，即在具有特别有利结构的特定代理函数（负熵）下证明相同结论，从而澄清相对光滑性条件下加速究竟在何种程度上不可能。

Method: 通过构造硬实例（hard instance）来证明下界：在标准d-单纯形上，对于关于负熵相对L-光滑的凸函数类别，证明任意一阶方法的收敛率至少为Ω(L/T)，要求d=Ω(T²)。证明结构上利用了负熵的结构特性（包括对称性和强凸性等），并进一步将证明技术推广到量子场景，针对谱面体上的负冯·诺依曼熵建立类似低界。

Result: 1) 建立了Ω(L/T)的一阶方法收敛率下界，适用于关于负熵相对光滑的凸函数类别；2) 证明了镜像下降在该类别中阶数最优（仅差对数因子）；3) 将结果扩展到量子设置，对关于负冯·诺依曼熵相对光滑的函数在谱面体上得到同样的下界。

Conclusion: 即使代理函数（负熵）具有特别良好的结构，在相对光滑假设下加速仍然不可能实现。这一结果对于经典优化和量子优化的情境都成立，说明相对光滑性的框架本质上限制了加速方法的可能性。

Abstract: We prove an $Ω(L/T)$ lower bound for the convergence rate of minimization in the class of functions that are convex and $L$-smooth relative to negative entropy on the standard $d$-simplex, valid for every first-order method when $d = Ω(T^2)$. In particular, this shows that mirror descent is optimal up to a logarithmic factor in this class. This may be surprising due to the fact that accelerated methods are readily available under the assumption of smoothness in $\ell_1$-norm. While Dragomir et al. (Mathematical Programming, 2022) have already showed that acceleration might be impossible under relative smoothness, their prox-function is pathological and constructed together with the hard instance. In contrast, we show non-acceleration for a specific prox-function with particularly favorable structure. We also extend the result to the quantum setting, proving the same lower bound in the class of functions $L$-smooth relative to negative von Neumann entropy on the spectrahedron of $d \times d$ Hermitian positive-semidefinite matrices with unit trace.

</details>


### [235] [Branching out: Prognostics-Based Replacement Policies for Series Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27899)
*Daniel Koutas,Daniel Straub*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于预测的混合规划方法用于制定预测性维护策略，考虑了决策选项、预测模型提供的系统未来状态信息以及更新-报酬过程的成本。该方法生成的策略仅由少数参数定义，可通过理论推导或运行至故障数据优化确定，在预防性更换和预防性订购两种决策场景中均表现出与优化基准策略相当的性能，同时更高效且对过拟合更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的预测性维护策略设计往往过于复杂或对数据过拟合，需要一种既能够充分利用预测模型的未来状态信息、又足够简洁高效且稳健的混合规划方法，以在实际工程中制定可操作且性能优良的维护决策策略。

Method: 提出一种混合规划方法，融合三方面要素：(1) 可用的决策选项；(2) 预测模型提供的系统未来状态信息；(3) 更新-报酬过程的成本。策略由少数参数定义，参数可通过理论推导或从运行至故障数据中优化求解获得。

Result: 在预防性更换和预防性订购两个预测性维护决策场景中进行了数值验证。结果表明，所提出的策略在性能上与优化后的基准策略相媲美，同时在计算效率和抗过拟合能力上具有显著优势。

Conclusion: 该混合规划方法能够以极简的参数化策略形式实现与复杂优化基准策略相当的预测性维护性能，兼具高效率和鲁棒性，为理论分析和实际部署提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We propose a hybrid planning method for deriving prognostics-based predictive maintenance policies. The method accounts for the available decision options, the information on the future state of the system provided by a prognostic model, and the costs of the underlying renewal-reward process. It results in policies defined by only a few parameters, which can be determined based on theoretical considerations or by optimization from run-to-failure data. We demonstrate the potential of the method in two separate predictive maintenance decision settings: preventive replacement and preventive ordering. Numerical investigations show that the derived policies rival the performance of optimized benchmark policies, while being significantly more efficient and robust against overfitting.

</details>


### [236] [The Existence and Stability of Generalized Multi-Source Weber Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27900)
*V. S. T. Long,N. M. Nam,T. H. Tai,C. Tammer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了最小时间函数框架下具有集值目标的多源Weber问题，建立了全局与局部最优解的存在性，分析了解集的闭性、紧性、有界性等定性性质，推导了目标函数和最优值函数关于目标集扰动的Lipschitz连续性，并研究了全局与局部解映射的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多源Weber问题（即多源选址问题）在设施选址、物流优化等领域有广泛应用。当目标为集值（set-valued）而非单点时，问题的数学结构和灵敏度分析变得更为复杂。本文旨在最小时间函数的统一框架下对广义多源Weber问题进行全面研究，填补已有理论结果在解的存在性、定性性质及稳定性分析方面的空白。

Method: 本文采用最小时间函数（minimal time functions）作为研究框架，首先通过适当的假设证明全局和局部最优解的存在性；随后利用变分分析和集值分析的工具，研究解集的闭性、紧性、有界性/无界性的判别条件；接着推导目标函数和最优值函数关于目标集扰动（在合适的拓扑意义下）的Lipschitz连续性；最后引入全局与局部解映射，并借助集值分析的稳定性理论（如上半/下半连续性和闭性）研究其灵敏度性质。

Result: 本文获得了以下主要结果：（1）全局与局部最优解的存在性定理；（2）解集的闭性、紧性以及有界性/无界性条件；（3）目标函数与最优值函数关于目标集扰动的显式Lipschitz连续性性质；（4）全局与局部解映射的稳定性性质。这些结果为广义多源Weber问题提供了定性和定量的灵敏度分析。

Conclusion: 本文通过最小时间函数框架，系统性地建立了广义多源Weber问题的完整理论体系，涵盖了最优解存在性、解集结构性质以及解映射的稳定性。所得结果为该类问题在工程与运筹实践中的数值求解和鲁棒性分析奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper studies the generalized multi-source Weber problem with set-valued targets in the framework of minimal time functions. We first establish the existence of global and local optimal solutions and investigate several qualitative properties of the corresponding solution sets, including closedness, compactness, and conditions ensuring boundedness or unboundedness. Next, we derive explicit Lipschitz continuity properties for the objective function and the associated optimal value function with respect to perturbations of the target sets. We then introduce the global and local solution mappings and study their stability properties from the viewpoint of set-valued analysis. These results provide a quantitative and qualitative sensitivity analysis for the generalized multi-source Weber problem in the setting of minimal time functions.

</details>


### [237] [OptGraph: Large Language Models Enhanced Evolutionary Optimization Via Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27918)
*Xianchao Xiu,Jianhao Li,Huangyue Chen,Wanquan Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: OptGraph是首个引入图检索增强生成（GraphRAG）的优化代理工作流，通过构建类型化图结构来存储可复用的优化经验，在推理阶段利用图邻域信息丰富检索上下文，并支持自适应知识更新，在基准数据集上相比最先进的基于提示的自动优化框架平均精确度提升8.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型自动进化优化方法存在三个主要局限：模式复用能力不足、缺乏错误感知的细化机制、以及跨多样化任务的检索鲁棒性较差。这些问题限制了LLM在自动化优化场景中的效率和泛化能力。

Method: OptGraph提出了一种优化代理工作流，核心方法包括：(1) 将可复用的优化经验构建为类型化图，捕捉建模模式、问题形式化、实现细节和错误纠正之间的关系；(2) 在推理阶段利用图邻域信息增强检索知识，提供结构化上下文以改善建模、验证和迭代细化；(3) 支持自适应知识更新，通过将执行轨迹和验证反馈蒸馏为可复用的图知识，无需对LLM进行参数微调。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，OptGraph相比最先进的基于提示的自动优化框架，平均精确度提升8.9%。代码已开源。

Conclusion: OptGraph通过图结构化知识表示和GraphRAG机制有效解决了现有LLM自动优化方法在模式复用、错误感知细化及检索鲁棒性方面的不足，验证了图增强检索在自动进化优化任务中的有效性，为后续研究提供了可扩展的框架基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as a powerful tool for automated evolutionary optimization, but existing methods remain limited in pattern reuse, error-aware refinement, and retrieval robustness across diverse tasks. To address these limitations, we propose OptGraph, the first optimization agentic workflow that introduces graph retrieval-augmented generation (GraphRAG). Specifically, OptGraph first constructs reusable experience as a typed graph, capturing the relationships among modeling patterns, problem formalization, implementation details, and error corrections. In the inference stage, OptGraph leverages graph neighborhood information to enrich retrieved knowledge, providing structured context to improve modeling, verification, and iterative refinement. Moreover, OptGraph supports adaptive knowledge updates, enabling the distillation of execution traces and verification feedback into reusable graph knowledge without ndertaking LLM parameter tuning. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets show that our proposed OptGraph achieves an average exact accuracy 8.9% higher than the state-of-the-art prompt-based automated optimization frameworks. Our code has been made available at https://github.com/xianchaoxiu/OptGraph.

</details>


### [238] [Global exponential turnpike properties for optimal control of the viscous Burgers equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28042)
*Emmanuel Trélat,Xingwu Zeng,Can Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对一维粘性Burgers方程，在局部内部控制作用下，建立了二次最优跟踪问题的全局指数turnpike性质。对于任意初始数据，当周期跟踪目标充分小时，有限时域最优解趋近唯一的最优周期状态；零目标情形则给出全局稳态turnpike（原点上），且无需对初始数据施加小性假设。这是粘性Burgers方程首个全局指数turnpike结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粘性Burgers方程是非线性偏微分方程控制理论中的核心模型，但此前关于其最优控制问题的指数turnpike性质仅有局部结果，缺乏全局性结论。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立无初始数据小性条件限制的全局turnpike性质。

Method: 证明结合两种关键技术：（1）通过严格凸性和周期Riccati理论建立局部指数turnpike性质；（2）利用抛物型耗散论证提供与时间区间长度无关的吸收时间（absorbing time），从而将局部结果推广为全局turnpike性质。

Result: 建立了全局指数turnpike性质：对任意初始数据，有限时域最优解指数逼近唯一最优周期轨线（目标充分小时）；零目标情形下实现全局稳态turnpike（收敛到原点），无需对初始数据施加小性假设。这是粘性Burgers方程的首个全局指数turnpike结果。

Conclusion: 本文首次为粘性Burgers方程建立了全局指数turnpike性质，通过局部turnpike与抛物耗散吸收时间相结合的策略成功克服了非线性带来的全局性困难，为该类非线性抛物系统的最优控制turnpike理论提供了新的方法论。

Abstract: We establish global exponential turnpike properties for quadratic optimal tracking problems governed by the one-dimensional viscous Burgers equation with localized internal control. For every initial datum, finite-horizon optimal solutions approach the unique optimal periodic regime when the periodic tracking target is sufficiently small; the zero-target case yields a global steady turnpike at the origin, with no smallness assumption on the initial datum. To our knowledge, these are the first global exponential turnpike results for the viscous Burgers equation. The proof combines a local exponential turnpike, obtained through strict convexity and periodic Riccati theory, with a parabolic dissipation argument that provides an absorbing time independent of the horizon.

</details>


### [239] [Multi-Asset Utility Maximization with Jump Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28051)
*Sigui Brice Dro*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了多维布朗运动和独立齐次泊松随机测度驱动的风险资产下的投资组合效用最大化问题，允许策略包含跳跃信号。采用鞅最优性原理推导带跳的BSDE来刻画值函数和最优策略，证明了BSDE解的存在唯一性，处理了异质跳跃信号的特殊情形，并对对数效用情形下的高斯信号进行了数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融投资组合优化中，现实市场中的资产价格不仅受布朗运动驱动，还受跳跃事件影响（如突发事件、信息冲击）。投资者可能获得跳跃信号来优化策略，但多维情形下带跳跃信号的投资组合效用最大化问题尚未被充分研究。

Method: 首先将组合动态表示为半鞅过程；然后利用鞅最优性原理推导出带跳的BSDE，通过其解来刻画值函数和最优策略；继而证明该带跳BSDE解的存在唯一性；针对投资者接收异质跳跃信号的特殊情形进行专门处理；最后对对数效用情形下的高斯信号进行数值模拟验证。

Result: 成功将一维指数效用情形的框架推广到多维跳跃设定，证明了相关带跳BSDE解的存在唯一性，给出了最优策略的显式刻画，并通过高斯信号的对数效用数值算例验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了多维布朗运动和跳跃过程共同驱动下、允许跳跃信号时的投资组合效用最大化问题的完整理论框架，为实际投资决策中利用跳跃信息提供了理论基础和数值支持。

Abstract: In this paper, we study portfolio utility maximization problem in a setting where the risky asset is driven by a multidimensional Brownian motion and an independent homogeneous Poisson random measure, and where strategies may incorporate jump signals. Following the same approach as in the one-dimensional case for the exponential utility function [17], we first represent the portfolio dynamics as semimartingale processes. We then use martingale optimality principle to derive the corresponding backward stochastic differential equation (BSDE) with jumps to characterize both value function and an optimal strategy in terms of its solution. We subsequently prove the existence and uniqueness of the solution to the related BSDE with jumps. We also address the idiosyncratic case i.e where the investor receive heterogeneous jump signals. Finally, we provide numerical illustrations with Gaussian signals for the logarithmic case.

</details>


### [240] [On Leader Selection for Strong Structural Controllability in Matrix-Weighted Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28168)
*Lanhao Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文解决矩阵加权网络中最小领导者集选择的逆合成问题，证明结构不可控性仅源于维度特异性可达性隔离和拓扑对称等价，并提出两阶段综合框架（可达性先决条件+三种对称破缺算法）以确保强结构可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在矩阵加权网络中，选择最小领导者集以保证强结构可控性（SSC）是一个尚未解决的NP-hard难题。该问题的解决对网络控制理论具有重要意义，但现有方法缺乏严格的数学框架来识别和克服结构不可控的根本瓶颈。

Method: 提出两阶段综合方法：(1) 可达性先决条件阶段，识别结构根节点；(2) 三种对称破缺算法，包括贪心Weisfeiler-Lehman选择、子模界最大化和分区熵最大化。通过数学证明保证对不变子空间和结构膨胀的免疫性。

Result: 通过严格的数学证明和跨多种拓扑的广泛数值评估，验证了所提框架的有效性，证明其对不变子空间和结构膨胀问题具有免疫性，能够在各种网络拓扑中有效选择最小领导者集。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了矩阵加权网络中最小领导者集选择问题的严格数学框架，为NP-hard困难下的结构可控性综合提供了理论保障和实用算法。

Abstract: The inverse synthesis problem of selecting a minimal leader set to guarantee strong structural controllability (SSC) in matrix-weighted networks remains an unresolved NP-hard challenge. This paper proposes a rigorous mathematical framework to solve this. We prove that structural uncontrollability stems exclusively from dimension-specific reachability isolation and topological symmetry equivalence. To overcome these bottlenecks, we formulate a two-phase synthesis: a reachability prerequisite to identify structural roots, followed by three distinct symmetry-breaking algorithms (Greedy Weisfeiler-Lehman Selection, Submodular Bound Maximization, and Partition Entropy Maximization). Mathematical proofs guarantee immunity to invariant subspaces and structural dilation, validated by extensive numerical evaluations across diverse topologies.

</details>


### [241] [The optimality of an (s, S) hiring policy on a workforce planning problem with fixed recruitment costs and binomial turnover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28171)
*Zhen Chen,Roberto Rossi,Belen Martin-Barragan,S. Armagan Tarim*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究有限时间视野下的人员配置问题，员工流动遵循与招聘后人员水平相关的二项分布，提出一种带固定招聘成本的随机动态规划模型，证明最优策略呈(s,S)结构，并开发了基于分段近似的混合整数线性规划求解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人员配置模型通常假设人员流动与当前人员水平无关或过于简化，且固定招聘成本使问题难以分析。本文旨在处理决策依赖转移概率和固定招聘成本并存的复杂随机动态规划问题，为实际企业人员配置决策提供理论支撑和高效算法。

Method: 建立有限时域随机动态规划模型，其中每期员工离职服从参数依赖于招聘后人员水平的二项分布，并含固定招聘成本。引入"二项式K-凸性"(Binomial-K-convexity)概念，证明变量单期成本（期望工资与惩罚成本之和）的离散凸性及期望总成本的K-凸性在Bellman函数的二项式传播下得以保持。由此推导最优雇佣策略具有(s,S)型结构，并通过分段近似方法转化为混合整数线性规划(MILP)求解每周期的(s,S)参数。

Result: 证明了最优雇佣策略呈(s,S)型结构：人员水平低于阈值s时招聘至S，否则不招聘。数值实验表明，所提出的MILP方法计算速度快且最优性间隙小。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入二项式K-凸性这一新概念，成功刻画了具有决策依赖二项式流失和固定招聘成本的人员配置问题的最优策略结构，并提供了高效的计算求解方法，兼顾了理论严谨性与实际可行性。

Abstract: We study a finite-horizon workforce planning problem in which staff turnover in each period follows a binomial distribution whose parameters depend on the post-hiring workforce level. The model incorporates a fixed hiring cost that is incurred whenever recruitment occurs, regardless of the number of employees hired. The objective is to minimise the expected total cost, including recruitment, salary, and shortage costs, where deviations below period-specific staffing requirements are penalised. To analyse this stochastic dynamic programme with decision-dependent transition probabilities, we establish the discrete convexity of the variable single-period cost (the sum of expected salary and penalty costs) and the K-convexity of the expected total cost. Specifically, we introduce the concept of Binomial-K-convexity to facilitate the proof that K-convexity is preserved under Binomial propagation in the Bellman function. We then show that the optimal hiring policy exhibits an (s, S)-type structure: when the workforce level in a given period falls below a threshold s, staff are hired up to level S; otherwise, no hiring occurs. Furthermore, we develop a piecewise approximation approach that yields a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) formulation for solving the problem and computing the (s, S) parameters for each period. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves fast computation with small optimality gaps.

</details>


### [242] [No-gap second-order conditions for optimization problems involving transport distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28264)
*Nicolas Borchard,Christian Meyer,Gerd Wachsmuth*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了测度空间中的优化问题，以到给定先验测度的传输距离作为正则项，利用弱-*二阶次导数的理论推导无间隙型的二阶最优性条件，并在光滑目标函数和Kantorovich势的附加假设下证明其与二次增长的等价性，最后将结果应用于测度空间中的最优控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在测度空间中研究带有传输距离正则项的优化问题，需要建立无间隙（no-gap）的二阶最优性条件，这是稀缺且具有挑战性的理论问题。

Method: 利用弱-*二阶次导数理论推导二阶最优性条件；计算弱-*二阶次导数并证明弱-* epidifferentiability；在光滑目标和Kantorovich势的附加假设下建立与二次增长的等价关系。

Result: 推导出无间隙型的二阶最优性条件，证明了其与二次增长在附加假设下的等价性，并成功应用于测度空间中的最优控制问题。

Conclusion: 弱-*二阶次导数理论为带传输距离正则的测度空间优化问题提供了有效的二阶最优性分析框架，并在最优控制问题中得到了应用验证。

Abstract: We consider optimization problems in the space of measures. As a regularization term, the problem includes the transport distance to a given prior measure. For the derivation of second-order optimality conditions of no-gap type, the theory of weak-$\star$ second subderivatives is used which will lead to an equivalence with quadratic growth under additional assumptions on the smooth part of the objective and on the Kantorovich potential, i.e., the solution of the dual transport problem. Further, the weak-$\star$ second subderivative is calculated and weak-$\star$ epidifferentiability is proven. Finally, the results are applied to optimal control problems in measure space.

</details>


### [243] [Exact and Approximate Solvability of Systems Involving Proximity Operators in Hilbert Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28343)
*Mark Allien D. Roble*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了实Hilbert空间中有限个真、下半连续凸函数的近端算子方程组的存在性与唯一性，建立了近似解存在性的充要条件，并引入了逆近端性质（IPP）作为逆最佳逼近性质（IBAP）的推广，应用于可行性问题和信号恢复问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近端算子方程系统是经典投影问题的自然推广，在凸优化、信号处理等领域有广泛应用。现有的逆最佳逼近性质（IBAP）仅适用于投影映射，需要将其推广到更一般的近端算子情形，以解决更广泛的优化和反问题。

Method: (1) 建立近端算子方程组$(\forall i\in I)\ \mathrm{prox}_{f_i}(x)=p_i$的存在性与唯一性理论；(2) 推导近似解存在性的充要条件；(3) 引入逆近端性质（IPP）并给出其多种刻画，作为IBAP的推广；(4) 将理论结果应用于可行性问题和信号恢复问题。

Result: 给出了近端算子方程组精确解和近似解存在性的充要条件，建立了IPP的多种等价刻画，并在可行性问题和信号恢复问题中展示了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究将逆最佳逼近性质推广至逆近端性质，为Hilbert空间中涉及近端算子的优化与反问题提供了统一的理论框架，丰富了凸分析与变分分析的工具箱。

Abstract: Let $\HH$ be a real Hilbert space and let $(f_i)_{i\in I}$ be a finite family of proper, lower semicontinuous, and convex functions on $\HH$. This study investigates the existence and uniqueness of exact solutions to systems involving proximity operators of the form: \
  $(\forall i\in I)\ \pr{f_i}(x)=p_i,$
  where $(p_i)_{i\in I}$ is a prescribed collection of proximal points in $\HH$, which naturally generalize classical projection problems. We establish necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of approximate solutions to such systems. Moreover, we introduce and derive several characterizations of the inverse proximal property (IPP), as a generalization of the inverse best approximation property (IBAP). Applications of the obtained results are presented in the contexts of a feasibility problem and signal recovery problem, demonstrating the relevance of the proposed framework to optimization and inverse problems in Hilbert spaces.

</details>


### [244] [Beyond Hand-Derived Inequalities: Decision Diagrams for Cut Generation in Binary Polynomial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28511)
*Martin Cooper,Margarita Castro*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于决策图(DD)的切割平面生成方法，用于二元多项式优化(BPO)问题。该方法针对超图的多线性集，为任意选定的支撑结构分离出局部多线性多胞体的面定义割，并提升回原问题。文中还提出了紧凑的DD编码、扩展公式、宽度刻画（通过有效反链发现新的多项式可解超图类）以及切割面性质的证书。实验表明该方法在根节点获得更大间隙闭合且使用更少切割，显著加速分支定界过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的强不等式（如two-links、flowers、odd β-cycles）都是手工为固定支撑模式推导的，缺乏通用性和自动化能力。作者希望提出一种统一的、可自动化的切割生成框架，能够为任意支撑结构分离面定义割，克服手工推导的局限性。

Method: 提出决策图(DD)方法：为一个选定的支撑集，通过递归公式构建紧凑DD编码，仅表示顶点变量并将其隐式编码到状态表示中，从而在局部多线性多胞体上分离面定义割。该DD编码还导出：(i)多线性多胞体的扩展公式；(ii)基于有效反链的宽度刻画，揭示新的多项式可解超图类；(iii)生成割的面性质证书。此外探索了三种支撑策略：重用(reuse)、扩展(expand)和划分(partition)局部结构为section hypergraphs。

Result: 实证结果表明，基于DD的方法在根节点实现了更大的间隙闭合，且使用的切割数量更少，相比现有方法显著加速了分支定界过程的收敛。

Conclusion: 决策图方法为二元多项式优化提供了一种通用、自动化的切割生成框架，取代了手工推导强不等式的做法。该方法不仅在切割质量上优于现有流程，还通过宽度刻画揭示了新的多项式可解超图类，并提供了面性质的数学证书，具有理论和实践双重价值。

Abstract: We study cutting-plane generation for binary polynomial optimization (BPO), whose feasible region is the multilinear set of a hypergraph. Strong inequalities for this set---such as two-links, flowers, and odd $β$-cycles---are classically hand-derived for fixed support patterns. Instead, we propose a decision-diagram (DD) approach: for any chosen support, it separates a facet-defining cut in the local multilinear polytope and lifts it back to the original problem. We utilize a novel compact DD encoding based on a recursive formulation that represents only the vertex variables and implicitly encodes the hyperedge variables inside the state representation. From this DD encoding, we also obtain: (i) an extended formulation of the multilinear polytope, (ii) a width characterization via valid antichains that uncovers a new polynomially solvable class of hypergraphs, and (iii) a certificate for the facetness of the generated cuts. We explore three different support strategies for cut generation that reuse, expand, or partition local structures into section hypergraphs. Our empirical results show that our DD-based methodologies achieve a larger gap closure at the root node with fewer cuts than existing procedures and, in turn, markedly accelerate branch-and-bound procedures.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [245] [More Data, Worse Decisions? Preference Reversals in Neural Networks under Gram Incompatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27255)
*Yanli Yan,Yuanzheng Li,Yong Zhao,Hongbo Guo,Shoudong Han*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究固定表示神经网络在合并数据（池化重拟合）后是否保持源数据所支持的行为排序，即基于案例决策理论（CBDT）的合成公理是否成立。作者推导了偏好保持的精确与近似条件，引入尺度不变的Gram失配度量与面向几何的正则化，并提出三阶段审计流程，在负荷投标代理、医疗和金融决策代理上验证了稳定与易反转两种池化模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络越来越多地合并跨人群、跨时期、跨工况的数据以提升泛化能力，但合并后重新拟合的模型是否保留两个数据源共同支持的行为排序，这一可靠性问题此前缺乏系统研究。CBDT的合成公理形式化了这一要求，但该性质对固定表示神经网络是否成立尚未被分析。

Method: (1) 理论分析：证明池化重拟合实质上是重算用于加权源证据的逆Gram几何，可能导致共享偏好反转，并推导出偏好的精确保持与近似保持条件；(2) 度量与正则化：提出尺度不变的Gram失配度量用于候选池排序，以及面向几何的正则化用于在训练中塑造源几何结构；(3) 审计流程：开发三阶段审计，从严格的成对反转追踪到决策变化，再到任务定义的效用损失。

Result: 在负荷投标、医疗和金融决策代理上的实验揭示了稳定池化与反转易发池化两种模式：负荷审计在代理效用下识别出可测量的非零类源共识相悖的有害决策；跨域审计显示可比失配度可对应截然不同的保持率；面向几何的目标族在描述准确性、一致性、几何和危害四个维度上占据不同的操作点。

Conclusion: 该框架通过筛选、解析认证、面向几何的训练和决策后果审计，使合成可靠性变得可测量且可操作，为跨源池化模型提供了系统性的可靠性保障手段。

Abstract: Neural networks increasingly combine data across populations, time periods, and operating conditions to improve generalization. This raises a reliability question: whether a model refitted on pooled data preserves an action ordering supported by both sources. Case-Based Decision Theory (CBDT) formalizes this requirement through its composition axiom, which requires source-supported preferences to survive their union. We study when this property holds for fixed-representation neural networks with ordinary least squares (OLS) output heads. First, we show that pooled refitting recomputes the inverse-Gram geometry used to weight source evidence, which can reverse shared preferences, and derive exact and approximate preservation conditions. Next, we introduce a scale-invariant Gram mismatch measure for prioritizing candidate pools and geometry-oriented regularization for shaping source geometry during training. Finally, we develop a three-stage audit that traces strict pairwise reversals through decision changes to task-defined utility loss. Experiments spanning a load-based bidding proxy and medical and financial decision proxies reveal stable and reversal-prone pooling regimes: the load audit identifies a measurable nonzero class of source-consensus-relative harmful decisions under the proxy utility, while cross-domain audits show that comparable mismatch can correspond to sharply different preservation rates. Geometry-oriented objectives occupy distinct descriptive accuracy-consistency-geometry-harm operating points. Together, the framework makes compositional reliability measurable and operational through screening, analytic certification, geometry-oriented training, and decision-consequence auditing.

</details>


### [246] [Expected Survival-Time Bounds for Robust Optimization Over Time under Isotropic Gaussian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27280)
*Pavel Novoa-Hernández*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从理论角度研究鲁棒优化随时间(ROOT)中的期望生存时间问题，针对各向同性高斯环境动力学下固定部署解的生存时间，推导出严格下界和可计算的多步上界，并通过蒙特卡洛研究验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROOT领域虽然快速发展，但多数贡献停留在算法和实证层面，缺少理论基础。生存时间作为衡量时域鲁棒性的重要指标，其期望值与环境动力学、部署质量和问题特征的关系尚未被理解。

Method: 将生存问题建模为离散首次退出问题，在各向同性高斯环境动力学假设下，推导期望生存时间的严格下界和可计算的多步上界。

Result: 分析表明，在缓慢变化环境中期望生存时间以Θ(σ^{-2})尺度增长；在高维情形下趋近于最小值(即一个未来环境变化)。蒙特卡洛研究验证了理论预测，并检验了对建模假设和参数不确定性的敏感性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了部署生命周期的解析刻画，能够判断所需部署时域是否可保证、能否排除或是否在解析上无法解决，从而为优化后的部署决策提供理论支撑。

Abstract: Robust Optimization Over Time (ROOT) is a recent branch of evolutionary dynamic optimization that seeks solutions capable of remaining effective across multiple consecutive environments. Unlike the traditional track-the-moving-optimum (TMO) paradigm, which reoptimizes after every environmental change, ROOT explicitly values persistence. Although the field has grown considerably, most contributions remain algorithmic and empirical, leaving several fundamental properties poorly understood from a theoretical perspective. One such property is survival time, defined as the number of future environments in which a deployed solution continues to satisfy a prescribed quality threshold. While survival time is widely used as a measure of temporal robustness, little is known about how its expected value depends on environmental dynamics, deployment quality, or problem characteristics. This paper studies expected survival time for a fixed deployed solution under isotropic Gaussian environmental dynamics. Modeling survival as a discrete first-exit problem, we derive a rigorous lower bound and a computable multi-step upper bound. The analysis shows that expected survival scales as $Θ(σ^-{2})$ in slowly varying environments and approaches its minimum value of one future change in high dimensions. A comprehensive Monte Carlo study validates the theoretical predictions, examines sensitivity to modeling assumptions and parameter uncertainty, and illustrates how the bounds can support deployment decisions after optimization. The resulting framework provides an analytical characterization of deployment lifetime and identifies when a required deployment horizon can be guaranteed, ruled out, or remains analytically unresolved.

</details>


### [247] [An analysis of binary isotonic regression: degrees of freedom and implications for calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27301)
*Raphael Rossellini,Rina Foygel Barber,Zhimei Ren,Jake A. Soloff*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对二元样本上的保序回归（Isotonic Regression）给出了完全尖锐的有限样本自由度刻画，识别出最大化不同拟合值数量的二元序列；利用解析数论得到了以 3/(4π²)^{1/3} n^{2/3} 为首项的尖锐自由度上界，改进了此前结果。基于此确定性自由度界，推导出保序回归期望校准误差（ECE）的首个非平凡无分布保证，该保证完全免模型、免分布，仅假设 Y∈{0,1}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保序回归是估计单调函数和校准概率预测器的经典工具，但其在有限样本下的自由度（即模型复杂度）缺乏完全尖锐的刻画；同时，校准是概率预测的核心要求，而现有对保序回归期望校准误差（ECE）的保证要么依赖模型假设，要么需要分布假设，缺乏非平凡的无分布结果。

Method: （1）通过识别使保序回归产生最多不同拟合值的二元序列，给出有限样本下自由度的完全尖锐刻画；（2）利用解析数论工具推导自由度的尖锐上界，其首项为 3/(4π²)^{1/3} n^{2/3}；（3）将确定性自由度界应用于校准问题，推导保序回归 ECE 的无分布上界，仅假设二元结果 Y∈{0,1}。

Result: 得到了二元样本上保序回归自由度最坏情形的完全尖锐有限样本刻画，并给出了首项系数精确的自由度上界 3/(4π²)^{1/3} n^{2/3}，优于此前已知界。在此基础上，首次获得保序回归 ECE 的非平凡分布无关保证。

Conclusion: 该工作将解析数论与保序回归自由度分析相结合，不仅完善了经典保序回归的有限样本理论，还首次为其校准性能提供了完全模型无关、分布无关的 ECE 保证，为概率预测校准提供了更强的理论支撑。

Abstract: Isotonic regression is a canonical tool for estimating monotone functions and calibrating probabilistic predictors. We provide a fully sharp finite-sample characterization of its worst-case degrees of freedom on binary samples. Specifically, we identify the binary sequences that maximize the number of distinct fitted values produced by isotonic regression. We develop a sharp bound on the degrees of freedom with a leading term of $\frac{3}{(4π^2)^{1/3}} n^{2/3}$ using analytic number theory, improving on previous bounds.
  We then apply this result to calibration. Calibration is a central requirement for probabilistic prediction, and isotonic regression is a widely used post-processing method for improving calibration. Building on deterministic degrees-of-freedom bounds, we derive, to our knowledge, the first nontrivial distribution-free guarantee on the Expected Calibration Error (ECE) of isotonic regression. This ECE bound is fully model-free and distribution-free, only assuming $Y \in \{0,1\}$.

</details>


### [248] [HOMER: Huber-of-Means for Efficient and Robust Estimation in Hilbert Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27532)
*Kisung You,Boram Cho*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出HOMER（Huber-of-Means for Efficient and Robust Estimation），一种用于重尾分布下均值估计的鲁棒高效聚合方法。HOMER通过径向Huber中心聚合块均值，在众数（median）鲁棒性与样本均值效率之间插值，建立了Hilbert空间多数定理和MOM阶偏差界，并在有限三阶矩下支持参数速率的均值推断。模拟表明其在重尾数据下稳定，在干净高斯数据下接近样本均值效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重尾分布显著削弱了关于经验均值的高置信控制；众数均值（MOM）方法缺乏向均值效率靠近的阈值机制。现有方法在鲁棒性和效率之间存在难以调和的矛盾，需要一种能在两者间灵活插值的估计器。

Method: HOMER通过径向Huber中心聚合块均值。包含两种形式：canonical HUBER在二次区域内可恢复样本均值；pseudo-HUBER随阈值增长趋近样本均值。在Hilbert空间中建立多数定理和MOM阶偏差界（仅需有限二阶矩）。规范形式在二次区域内恢复样本均值；伪Huber形式随阈值增大趋近样本均值，并具有渐近线性性和一致的sandwich协方差估计。在有限三阶矩下，固定有限维投影支持以通常参数速率进行均值推断，要求块大小和块数量同时增长且块大小增长更快。

Result: 重尾模拟表明，当少数块摘要被偏移时HOMER保持稳定；在干净高斯数据上，两种版本均接近经验均值的效率。有限块的sandwich区间存在欠覆盖问题，尤其在偏斜的函数型数据上。当污染影响大多数块或破坏普通块内均值时，方法失败。

Conclusion: HOMER在鲁棒性与均值效率之间提供了可调谐的插值机制，理论上建立了在Hilbert空间中的偏差保证与参数速率推断条件，但有限样本下对sandwich区间覆盖率和极端污染场景的鲁棒性仍有局限，需进一步改进。

Abstract: Heavy tails weaken high-confidence control for the empirical mean. Geometric median-of-means (MOM) also lacks a threshold that moves toward mean efficiency. We propose \emph{HOMER}, or Huber-of-Means for Efficient and Robust Estimation. HOMER aggregates block means through a radial Huber center. Its canonical and pseudo-Huber forms bound each block score and interpolate between median-like robustness and the empirical mean. We establish a Hilbert-space majority theorem and a MOM-order deviation bound under a finite second moment. Canonical HOMER recovers the sample mean inside its quadratic region. Pseudo-HOMER approaches the sample mean as the threshold grows. It also admits asymptotic linearity and consistent sandwich covariance estimation around the population block-Huber target. Under a finite third moment, fixed finite-dimensional projections support mean inference at the usual parametric rate. This result requires growing block sizes and counts, with block sizes increasing faster. Heavy-tailed simulations show that HOMER remains stable when a minority of block summaries is displaced. On clean Gaussian data, both versions closely approach the empirical mean's efficiency. Finite-block sandwich intervals undercovered, especially for skewed functional data. Further studies show failure when contamination affects most blocks or compromises ordinary within-block means.

</details>


### [249] [Robust Wavelength Selection for Partial Least Squares Sugar Content Estimation Using Combinatorial Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27645)
*Mitsunobu Kanebako,Ami S. Koshikawa,Masaru Hitomi,Takuro Tanaka,Mahito Chiba,Maiko Mori,Masayuki Ohzeki*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于贝叶斯优化的组合优化方法用于近红外光谱的波长区域选择，通过构建稀疏二次代理模型并结合Thompson采样与模拟/量子退火求解QUBO问题，提升了偏最小二乘回归的预测精度和波长选择的一致性，且选出的波长区域具有局部稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近红外光谱分析中波长选择是重要的预处理步骤，可提升预测精度和可解释性。然而，波长区域选择本质上是二进制黑箱组合优化问题，现有方法（如遗传算法、模拟退火）存在预测精度不高、波长选择结果不一致以及容易陷入过拟合解等问题，因此需要一个更稳健高效的选择框架。

Method: 将波长区域选择建模为二进制黑箱优化问题，采用贝叶斯优化框架：构建稀疏二次代理模型对目标函数进行近似，通过Thompson采样顺序选取感兴趣的波长区域，将最小化采集函数转化为二次无约束二进制优化（QUBO）问题，并分别通过模拟退火和量子退火求解。

Result: 实验表明该方法相比基于遗传算法和模拟退火的波长选择方法，提升了偏最小二乘回归的预测精度，且选出的波长区域更具一致性。在一比特局部扰动下，验证集上均方根误差波动极小，表明所选波长区域具有良好的局部稳定性，算法收敛到了更平滑的误差曲面而非孤立的过拟合解。

Conclusion: 组合贝叶斯优化是光谱预测任务中进行稳健特征选择的有效框架，能够兼顾预测精度、选择一致性和局部稳定性。

Abstract: Wavelength selection is one of the important preprocessing methods in near-infrared spectroscopy to improve prediction accuracy and interpretability of spectral data. We formulate wavelength-region selection for sugar content estimation as a binary black-box optimization problem and propose a method based on Bayesian optimization. The proposed method constructs a sparse quadratic surrogate model and sequentially extracts interested wavelength regions by Thompson sampling. Minimizing an acquisition function is performed as a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization problem by simulated or quantum annealing. Experiments show that the proposed method improves the prediction accuracy of partial least squares regression and yields more consistent wavelength regions than genetic-algorithm-based selection and simulated annealing. Under one-bit local perturbations, the selected wavelength regions show minimal fluctuations in root mean square errors between observed and predicted values of a validation set. This local stability suggests that our method converges to a smoother error landscape and avoids isolated overfitted solutions. These results indicate that combinatorial Bayesian optimization is a useful framework for robust feature selection in spectroscopic prediction tasks.

</details>


### [250] [Error Analysis of Neural-Network-Based Engression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27723)
*Juntong Chen,Zijian Guo,Xinwei Shen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对基于能量分数的深度学习回归方法（Engression）进行了理论误差分析，将超额风险分解为近似误差、随机误差和蒙特卡洛误差三个部分，并在目标条件生成器具有组合光滑结构的假设下建立了收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Engression方法通过能量分数（一种严格适当的评分规则）来学习条件分布，已在实践中取得良好效果，但缺乏严格的理论误差分析。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为该方法提供可靠的统计学理论基础。

Method: 将Engression的超额风险分解为三个组成部分：近似误差（approximation error，反映网络表达能力）、随机误差（stochastic error，反映样本估计误差）和蒙特卡洛误差（Monte Carlo error，反映对能量分数中期望的采样近似误差）。基于此分解，在目标条件生成器满足组合光滑性（compositional smoothness）结构的假设下，推导出收敛速率。

Result: 建立了Engression在深度神经网络实现下的理论收敛速率。通过将超额风险分解为近似误差、随机误差和蒙特卡洛误差三个部分，在组合光滑性假设下给出了完整的误差界。

Conclusion: 该工作为Engression提供了严格的理论支撑，证明了其在合理假设下能够以可量化的速率收敛到真实条件分布，深化了对基于能量分数的生成式条件分布学习方法的理论理解。

Abstract: Engression (Shen and Meinshausen, 2024) learns a conditional distribution by fitting a generative model $Y = f(X,\varepsilon)$ under the energy score, a strictly proper scoring rule. We provide a theoretical error analysis of engression implemented with deep neural networks. We decompose the excess risk into three components: the approximation error, the stochastic error, and the Monte Carlo error. Based on this decomposition, we establish convergence rates under the assumption that the target conditional generator admits a compositional smoothness structure.

</details>


### [251] [Robust Estimation of Sparse Numerical Vectors under Local Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27815)
*Puning Zhao,Zhikun Zhang,Shaowei Wang,Sheng Yue,Bangzhou Xin,Tianhang Zheng,Pengfei Zhang,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出RPC（Randomized Projection with Clipping）方法，用于解决多物品用户场景下本地差分隐私（LDP）协议的投毒攻击防御问题。该方法通过随机投影与裁剪，配合基于精确偏差分析的校正技术，在无需偏差-方差权衡的情况下缩小输出空间，从而增强鲁棒性，并提供严格的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LDP协议容易受到投毒攻击，已有防御策略仅针对单物品用户。在实际场景中用户可能拥有多个物品，此时更大的输出空间使攻击者能够实施更强大的攻击而不被检测，这使得多物品用户的投毒攻击防御更具挑战性。

Method: 提出RPC（Randomized Projection with Clipping）方法：1）服务器向每个用户发送随机二进制向量，用户将本地数据投影到该向量上并裁剪值以限制攻击者能力；2）通过精确分析推导出裁剪偏差的闭式表达式，提出校正方法，从而无需再依赖偏差-方差权衡；3）裁剪阈值可进一步降低以缩小输出空间，增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在可信环境下，RPC方法达到与现有方法相当或更优的性能，表明其本身就是一个高效估计器。在不可信环境下，RPC方法对投毒攻击具有显著更强的鲁棒性，并提供在所有可能攻击下的严格误差理论保证。

Conclusion: RPC方法通过精确的偏差校正消除了偏差-方差权衡的限制，既是一个高效的独立估计器，又能在多物品用户的LDP场景下有效抵御投毒攻击，为大规模隐私保护数据聚合提供了更稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Local differential privacy (LDP) protocols are vulnerable to poisoning attacks. Existing research have proposed efficient defense strategies for single-item users. However, in practice, a user may possess multiple items. The defense against poisoning attacks for multi-item users is challenging, because due to larger output spaces, the adversary can conduct more powerful attacks without being detected. In this paper, we address the robust sparse vector mean estimation problem, in which each user has a vector with $m$ nonzero coordinates. We propose Randomized Projection with Clipping (RPC). Firstly, the server sends a random binary vector to each user. The user then projects its local data on the vector, and clip the value to restrict the attacker's capability. To handle clipping bias, we propose a correction method based on a careful analysis that gives an exact expression of the bias. As a result, bias-variance tradeoff is no longer needed, thus the clipping threshold can be further reduced to shrink the output space and enhance robustness. We provide a rigorous theoretical guarantee of the estimation error under all possible attacks. Numerical experiments show that under trusted environments, our new method achieves comparable or better performance than existing methods, indicating that our method is already an efficient estimator in its own right. Under untrusted environments, our method is also significantly more robust to poisoning attacks.

</details>


### [252] [Generalization and Trade-off in Adversarial Training: An RKHS Perspective via Kernel Integral Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27995)
*Yiling Xie,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）框架下研究对抗训练的泛化行为，推导了源均匀泛化误差界和匹配下界，揭示了对抗训练相较于极小平方预测基准存在统计精度损失，并提出两阶段噪声去偏方法以恢复极小极大多项式速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练是抵御对抗攻击的主流方法，但其在非参数框架下的泛化特性尚不清晰，尤其在对抗鲁棒性与统计精度之间的权衡缺乏理论刻画。

Method: 借助核积分算子分析RKHS中的对抗训练估计器，推导源均匀泛化误差上界；在固定多项式谱模型上建立匹配下界；通过分解混合鲁棒项中的噪声贡献，提出两阶段噪声去偏（noise-debiased）估计程序。

Result: 证明了对抗训练的最优平衡泛化速率可比极小极大预测基准更慢，指出该损失源于对抗鲁棒性与观测噪声的交互——混合项中的噪声贡献虽降低了估计复杂度，却拖慢了逼近速率；所提出的两阶段去偏估计器可在适中选择鲁棒性水平时达到极小极大多项式速率（至多差对数因子）。

Conclusion: 研究在非参数框架下完整刻画了对抗训练的泛化行为，为对抗鲁棒性与泛化性能之间的权衡提供了新的理论解释与原则性解决方案，数值实验验证了理论发现和所提方法的有效性。

Abstract: Adversarial training has emerged as a powerful approach for protecting models against adversarial attacks in a broad range of real-world applications. In this paper, we study adversarial training in the reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) framework through the associated kernel integral operator. We first derive source-uniform generalization error bounds for the RKHS adversarial training estimator in terms of the robustness level, sample size, source smoothness, and kernel spectrum. On a fixed polynomial-spectrum model, we further establish a matching lower bound showing that the optimally balanced generalization rate can be slower than the minimax prediction benchmark. This result reveals a loss of statistical accuracy in adversarial training. Our analysis shows that this loss arises from the interaction between adversarial robustness and observation noise: the noise contribution in the mixed robustness term slows the approximation rate, although the same term reduces the estimation complexity. To address this limitation, we propose a two-stage noise-debiased procedure that estimates and removes the noise contribution from the mixed term. The resulting estimator improves the generalization rate and attains the minimax polynomial rate, up to a logarithmic factor, when the robustness level is selected at the stated sample-dependent order. Our results characterize the generalization behavior of adversarial training in a nonparametric framework and provide a new interpretation and a principled solution for the trade-off between adversarial robustness and generalization. Numerical experiments support the theoretical findings and demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [253] [On a joint simultaneous learning of relevant feature subsets and subspaces in regression-like problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28080)
*Illia Horenko*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出熵最优流形回归(EOMR)，扩展了熵最优流形聚类(EOMC)方法，使其能够联合识别非平稳、非线性回归问题中的相关特征子集和子空间。EOMR具有线性扩展的迭代和记忆复杂度，在混沌和流体动力学基准测试（Lorenz-96系统、Hasegawa-Wakatani托卡马克等离子体模型）中，相比梯度提升随机森林、深度神经网络及TabPFN等SOTA工具，预测误差低数个数量级，模型复杂度低数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EOMC方法仅适用于聚类问题，无法处理非平稳和非线性回归任务；而当前主流的机器学习/人工智能工具（如深度神经网络、Transformer模型）在混沌动力系统等高维小样本回归问题上表现不佳，预测误差大且模型复杂度高，迫切需要一种能够同时进行特征选择与子空间识别的高效回归方法。

Method: EOMR在熵最优流形框架下扩展回归能力，通过联合优化识别相关特征的子集和特征子空间，实现对非平稳非线性回归问题的鲁棒学习。方法在迭代和内存复杂度上均呈线性扩展，适用于高维数据的高效处理。

Result: 在Lorenz-96系统（F=8和F=12强混沌/超强混沌）和Hasegawa-Wakatani托卡马克等离子体数据上的实验表明：EOMR相比通用梯度提升随机森林、深度神经网络和TabPFN v.03等SOTA工具，均方根预测误差低数个数量级，模型复杂度低数个数量级。在Hasegawa-Wakatani案例中，EOMR仅用8个参数的线性因果弱平稳自回归过程即可精炼描述主导EOF动力学。

Conclusion: EOMR在混沌和流体动力学等极具挑战性的回归问题上显著优于现有SOTA机器学习方法和AI工具，兼具高预测精度、极低模型复杂度和线性计算复杂度，对高维小样本非平稳非线性问题具有突出优势，有望成为复杂动力学系统建模的更有效工具。

Abstract: We extend a recently introduced Entropy-Optimal Manifold Clustering (EOMC) to allow for a joint simultaneous identification of subsets and subspaces of relevant features in nonstationary and nonlinear regression problems. It is shown that the proposed extension - that we coin as Entropy-Optimal Manifold Regression (EOMR) - allows a robust learning with linearly-scaling iteration and memory complexities. EOMR is compared to the most complete set of state-of-the-art tools from the Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Machine Learning (ML) that is available to the author, on the very challenging problems from chaotic and fluid dynamics: (i) on predicting the Lorenz-96 systems dynamics in strongly- and very-strongly chaotic regimes (with forcing parameter being $F=8$ and $F=12$, respectively); and, (ii) on a data from the Hasegawa-Wakatani model on the edge of the tokamak plasma. It is demonstrated that the proposed benchmarks (i) and (ii), indeed, are the very challenging problems for the state of the art ML and AI tools - since both the general-purpose gradient boosted random forests and deep neuronal networks, as well as transformer-based AI tools like TabPFN v.03 (more spezialised for large-dimensional small data learning problems) - result in orders of magnitude inferior root mean squared prediction errors, and orders of magnitude larger model complexities, when compared to the EOMR. For a Hasegawa-Wakatani example, EOMR distills a very simple entropy-optimal and skilful description of the leading Essential Orthogonal Function (EOF) dynamics, given by linear, causal and weakly-stationary autoregressive process described by just 8 parameters.

</details>


### [254] [Uncertainty quantification for trustworthy deep learning: Methods and measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28248)
*H. Martin Gillis,Thomas Trappenberg*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提供了一篇关于深度学习不确定度量化（UQ）的结构化批判性综述，聚焦于基于集成和近似贝叶斯两类方法及其输出的汇总度量，系统覆盖五类方法族（贝叶斯神经网络、MC Dropout、深度集成、高效集成近似、最后一层/单次通过方法），并统一了"产生预测分布的方法"与"汇总不确定性的度量"之间的分离视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在安全关键领域的部署需要可靠的预测置信度估计，但传统网络架构缺乏原则性的不确定度量化手段。与现有UQ综述相比，本文旨在贡献更深入的集成高效近似与单次通过方法的剖析，并提供一个统一的分析框架，以弥合方法分类与度量总结之间的割裂，推动该领域更系统化的理解与发展。

Method: 本文将UQ方法组织为五大类：贝叶斯神经网络、MC Dropout、深度集成、高效集成近似、最后一层/单次通过方法。同时将相关工作（证据网络、先验网络、保形预测、事后校准）以及决策时任务（分布外检测、选择性预测）纳入讨论，逐一考察其理论动机、实现方式、实证表现与局限性；随后综述集成多样性理论和不确定度度量及其分解（对比熵分解与成对散度度量），统一评测方法以使定性比较具备共同基础，最后讨论大语言模型中的不确定度及开放研究方向。

Result: 本文完成了对五类UQ方法的系统梳理与批判性评估，建立了方法与度量分离的统一视角，整合了集成多样性理论、不确定度分解和评测方法论，并识别出高效分类情景下的认知度量、最后一层多样性、移位下的多样性与校准、混合架构等开放研究方向，以及大语言模型中不确定度处理的简要讨论。

Conclusion: 本文通过统一框架整合了UQ领域的主要方法族和度量体系，强调了方法（预测分布产生）与度量（不确定性汇总）分离的必要性和价值，指出了当前研究中的关键空白与未来方向，为该领域的系统化研究提供了基础性综述。

Abstract: The deployment of deep neural networks in safety-critical domains demands reliable estimates of predictive confidence, yet conventional architectures lack principled uncertainty quantification. This survey provides a structured, critical review of methods for Uncertainty Quantification (UQ) in deep learning, scoped to ensemble-based and approximate Bayesian approaches and the measures used to summarize their outputs. Relative to existing UQ surveys, our contribution is depth on efficient ensemble approximations and single-pass methods, and a unified treatment that separates the method producing a predictive distribution from the measure that summarizes its uncertainty. We organize methods into five families: Bayesian neural networks, Monte Carlo Dropout, deep ensembles, efficient ensemble approximations, and last-layer or single-pass approaches. We situate adjacent work on evidential and prior networks, conformal prediction, and post-hoc calibration, together with the decision-time tasks of out-of-distribution detection and selective prediction. For each, we examine theoretical motivation, implementation, empirical performance, and limitations. We then review ensemble diversity theory and uncertainty measures and their decompositions, contrasting the entropy decomposition with pairwise divergence measures, and consolidate evaluation methodology so that our qualitative comparisons share a common basis. We close with a brief treatment of uncertainty in large language models and open research directions, including efficient epistemic measures for classification, last-layer diversity, diversity and calibration under shift, and hybrid architectures.

</details>


### [255] [Doubly Robust Functional Representation Learning for Longitudinal Causal Inference with Irregular Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28567)
*Mengfei Ran,Yifeng Shen,Ruijie Guan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Doubly Robust Functional Representation Learning (DR-FRL)，一种交叉拟合工作流，将不规则功能纵向数据（如实验室值、生理信号等不均衡时序观测）转化为面向估计目标的表示状态，用于观测历史制度下的因果推断。该方法结合功能/时序编码器、多重干扰参数头、以及EIF靶向的诊断技术，确保表示误差仅进入二阶乘积余项，使均值估计量在新显式的速率、重叠、校准和稳定性条件下渐进线性。模拟与VitalDB实际数据验证表明其有效性与实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向因果研究常记录为不规则功能片段（实验室值、生理信号、图像衍生摘要等），在不均衡且信息性时间点上测量。标准的双重稳健估计器通常需要标量摘要，而序列学习器优化的是预测损失，无法稳定高效影响函数（EIF）。现有方法难以在不规则历史数据上同时兼顾双重稳健性和EIF稳定性，因此需要一种专门针对此类数据结构的估计方法。

Method: DR-FRL包含以下核心组成：(1) 交叉拟合的工作流程；(2) 功能编码器与时序编码器，分别将点云和先前历史映射为状态表示；(3) 干扰参数头估计结局、治疗和删失函数；(4) EIF靶向的验证、校准、重叠、尾部及消融诊断，评估状态是否支持估计方程；(5) 理论分析表明若选取的状态保留了EIF所需的信息，表示误差进入与普通干扰误差相同的二阶乘积余项；(6) Catoni聚合作为有界影响点估计单独处理，不替代Wald推断。

Result: 模拟实验表明，当功能混杂维度高、测量具有信息性、支持弱或伪结局重尾时，DR-FRL表现优于现有方法。VitalDB审计显示，DR-FRL可利用不规则的实验室点云数据得出有用的阴性结论：对于该ICU处置端点，标量实验室摘要已携带大部分与端点相关的信息。

Conclusion: DR-FRL提供了一种将不规则功能历史转化为可支持EIF估计方程的表示状态的系统方法，理论上保证均值估计量的渐进线性性质，实践上通过诊断工具验证表示质量。其双重稳健性和广泛的适用条件使其成为复杂纵向因果推断的有力工具，且实际应用展示了其发现阴性结果的能力，有助于避免过度建模。

Abstract: Longitudinal causal studies often record histories as irregular functional fragments: laboratory values, physiologic signals, sensor streams, and image-derived summaries measured at unequal and informative times. Standard doubly robust estimators usually require scalar summaries, whereas sequence learners optimize prediction losses that need not stabilize the efficient influence function. We propose Doubly Robust Functional Representation Learning (DR-FRL), a cross-fitted workflow that turns irregular histories into estimand-targeted states for observed-history regimes. Functional and temporal encoders map point clouds and prior histories into states; nuisance heads estimate outcome, treatment, and censoring functions; and EIF-targeted validation, calibration, overlap, tail, and ablation diagnostics assess whether the state supports the estimating equation. If the selected state preserves the nuisance information needed by the EIF, representation error enters the same second-order product remainder as ordinary nuisance error, and the mean estimator is asymptotically linear under explicit rate, overlap, calibration, and stability conditions. Catoni aggregation is treated separately as a bounded-influence point estimator, not a replacement for Wald inference. Simulations show gains when functional confounding is high-dimensional, measurement is informative, support is weak, or pseudo-outcomes are heavy-tailed. A VitalDB audit shows that DR-FRL can use irregular laboratory point clouds and deliver a useful negative finding: for this ICU-disposition endpoint, scalar laboratory summaries already carry much endpoint-relevant information.

</details>
